- UI shell: teal medical design system (oklch tokens, no blue/indigo), light/dark via next-themes, .hc high-contrast variant, custom scrollbars, prefers-reduced-motion; Inter + Noto Sans Arabic fonts; sidebar (md+) + bottom 6-tab bar (mobile, safe-area); sticky footer (min-h-screen flex, pb-24 clears fixed nav); framer-motion tab transitions; hydration-safe mounted flag via useSyncExternalStore; first-visit auto-seed (localStorage).
- Views: Today (adherence ring SVG, streak card, risk badge, smart-reminder callout, dose timeline with 44px Taken/Snooze/Skip), Medications (cards with bdi names, show-original toggle for instructions, interaction alert + "explain with assistant" cross-tab seed, 3-step add wizard with autocomplete + live interaction warning + time/day/color pickers), Assistant (agent chips, persistent safety banner, emergency dialog 101/911/112, quick-asks, chat with sources <details> panel), Reports (upload/analyze LLM, sample fill, list, detail dialog: summary, numbered findings with source quotes, warnings, readability badge, grounded Q&A with citations, raw text disclosure), Insights (stat cards, week summary w/ WoW delta, 56-cell heatmap, Recharts bar + risk line, hour-bucket bars), Settings (language, theme, 4-step font scale, high contrast, reduced-motion note, reset demo data, about/attribution).
- Fixed during verification: retrieval threshold 2→1 + generic-word stopwords (short Arabic questions failed retrieval); seed probabilities (streak now 6, adherence 89%, risk medium); setState-in-effect lint rule via useSyncExternalStore; ESLint ignores for upload/examples/skills; removed Prisma query logging.
- Verified with agent-browser (desktop 1440x900 + mobile 390x844): RTL↔LTR switch, dose marking, wizard end-to-end (autocomplete, live warning, save → 4 interactions detected), assistant LLM reply in Arabic with citations, emergency guardrail instant fixed message, report analysis (LLM JSON), grounded Q&A with [1] citation + correct NOT_FOUND, dark mode, font scale (16→18px), footer sticky & clears mobile nav. VLM review: desktop 9/10, mobile 9/10. Lint + tsc clean for src/. All API routes 200 in dev.log.

Stage Summary:
- Deliverable: bilingual (ar/en) RTL-first MedSync AI prototype at / — implements WP1 IA (Today/Medications/Assistant/Reports/Insights/Settings) + WP4 Arabic/RTL/accessibility + working WP2/WP3 previews (heuristic risk, grounded RAG-lite Q&A with citations, curated interaction table, emergency guardrails).

- FIXED original-repo bug #1 (Mark Taken dead): original handleStatusToggle called medicineStatus(medId) with NO status; the original statusMedicineController rejects that with 400 "Invalid status" — so the button never worked upstream. Fix: pass 'taken' explicitly in Dashboard.jsx (documented in code comment). Verified: UI "1 of 6" → "3 of 6" + DB doses flipped to taken.
- FIXED original-repo bug #2 (mobile agents page broken): sidebarOpen initialized true on ALL screen sizes; the fixed w-72 (288px) sidebar left ~100px for chat on a 390px phone, truncating all text (VLM review flagged FAIL). Fix: initialize collapsed below lg breakpoint (window.innerWidth >= 1024). VLM re-review: PASS; desktop still starts open; mobile toggle verified both ways.
- FIXED port defect #3 (socket listener lifecycle): socketContext's no-token branch connected the socket on 'medsync-auth-changed' but never registered the onNotification listener (toasts only appeared after a Fast Refresh remount), and logout kept the socket alive (original logout did a full page unload; hash-router port doesn't). Fix: authReady STATE drives the whole effect (connect + listener + interval, cleanup disconnects); logout paths (Dashboard, Navbar) now dispatch 'medsync-auth-changed'. Verified: Test Notify → exactly ONE toast rendered, zero duplicate-key React warnings.
- FIXED port defect #4 (double delivery hardening): socketService.connect() created a NEW socket when an old one existed in connecting state, orphaning it (both sockets in the user room → every notification delivered twice → duplicate React keys). Fix: reuse existing socket; plus id-dedupe guard in the notification callback.
- Non-issues confirmed as ORIGINAL behavior (no change): GET /api/languages/user 400 before login (original controller also returns "User id missing"; client has a designed fallback to 'en' + supported list); "medications []" debug log (stale-closure console.log in original Dashboard); Analytics back-arrow navigate(-1) returning to landing.
- Final checks: bun run lint → 0 errors/0 warnings; dev.log → 0 5xx, all routes 200/201; VLM screenshot review: desktop dashboard PASS, mobile dashboard PASS (minor note), mobile agents PASS after fix.

Stage Summary:

Fix: scoped hide transforms below lg → `ltr:max-lg:-translate-x-full rtl:max-lg:translate-x-full lg:translate-x-0`. Stage Summary:

Each has: own ChatGroq chat model + own ChatGroq summary model, own summaryPrompt, per-user ConversationSummaryMemory Map (cap 200 — the ONE deliberate deviation from the original's global singleton, to prevent cross-user context leaks in a medical app), LLMChain primary path, z-ai fallback path with the identical rendered prompt.

NOT the primary backend: it only answers when Groq/Gemini are unreachable (region-blocked networks like this sandbox). On the user's own machine the primary providers answer and the fallback never runs.

Deleted the old centralized files: src/lib/server/agents.ts, src/lib/server/agent-route.ts, src/lib/server/llm.ts (grep confirmed zero remaining consumers).

- translation.ts: now uses ChatGoogleGenerativeAI (Gemini) as primary — the "Gemini" half of the original Groq/Gemini split (the original's Google Translate v2 endpoint rejects API-key auth, so Gemini is the faithful Google path); z-ai fallback when region-blocked; medical-terminology placeholder pipeline, cache, retries and {success, translatedText, originalText} contract untouched.

Task ID: 15
Agent: full-stack-developer
Task: ML model selection — Random Forest + Gradient Boosting with honest comparison/promotion (plan §16, §20–22)

Work Log:
- Read worklog (Task 10 MEDSYNC-007 section; Task 12 not present in worklog — last entry is 11-verify, numbering gap noted), then fully read features.ts / models.ts / train.ts / predict.ts / metrics.ts + the three API routes before touching anything.
- models.ts (baseline + LR code untouched): added exported `mulberry32(seed)` seeded RNG (default seed 42, zero Math.random in training); RandomForestModel/RFTree/RFTreeNode — greedy binary splits minimizing weighted Gini, midpoint thresholds quantile-thinned to ≤16/feature, maxDepth 4, minLeafSize 2, seeded ceil(sqrt(d)) feature subsample per node, Laplace-smoothed leaf probs (pos+1)/(n+2); forest = 60 trees each on a class-balanced bootstrap (n/2 pos + n/2 neg with replacement, uniform fallback if a class is empty); `randomForestPredict` = mean tree prob clamped [0.01,0.99] rounded 4; `randomForestImportance` = per-feature summed weighted-Gini reduction normalized to 1, sorted desc. GradientBoostingModel/GBTree — logistic loss, base = clamped train log-odds, 60 rounds × depth-2 MSE regression trees on residuals (same threshold thinning + seeded feature subsample), leaf = mean residual clamped [-2,2], F += 0.1·tree; `gbPredict` = sigmoid(F) clamped; `gbImportance` = per-feature accumulated split MSE (SSE) reduction normalized to 1. Everything JSON-serializable; constructors `trainRandomForest` / `trainGradientBoosting` mirror the LR signature style; trees work on raw features.
- train.ts: all four candidates (baseline, LR, rf-v{stamp}, gb-v{stamp}) trained on the SAME temporal 70/30 train split and evaluated on the SAME test split; TrainReport gained `datasetType: "real-user-demo"` (§18 honesty constant), `candidates[]` in fixed display order (baseline → logistic-regression → gradient-boosting → random-forest) and `randomForest` / `gradientBoosting` fields mirroring `logisticRegression` (old fields kept). Promotion: among candidates that beat-or-tie the baseline test ROC-AUC pick best ROC-AUC → tie-break lower Brier → tie-break simplicity order; lowData → baseline stays with insufficient-data reason (candidates evaluated/reported, NOT promoted); nothing beats baseline → baseline with honest reason; promoted reason appends "Small demo dataset — results are pipeline validation, not clinical evidence." when resolvedEvents < 200. Persisted one artifact per candidate (upsert; isActive only for the promoted one) and generalized the stale-active deactivation loop to ALL FOUR modelTypes (model versioning).
- predict.ts: ActiveModelCache + getActiveModel() now parse/handle all four modelTypes (kind-checked JSON parse, baseline fallback on parse failure — previous behavior preserved); scoreDoseEvent dispatches to logisticPredict / randomForestPredict / gbPredict / baselineScore; `explain()` returns GLOBAL feature importance (explanationKind "global-importance", contribution = importance [0..1], value/valueEn/valueAr via existing formatValue, labels from FEATURE_LABELS) for random-forest/gradient-boosting, unchanged signed per-dose contributions (explanationKind "per-dose") for baseline/LR; PredictionResult gained `explanationKind`; persistPrediction unchanged and type-agnostic (modelVersion string from artifact). DISCLAIMER_* and ExplanationItem interface preserved (only ADDITIVE field changes).
- API routes: train route header comment updated to describe the four-candidate comparison (code unchanged — response shape already generic over the report); status route untouched (already generic over artifacts).
- Constraints honored: pure TS, no new packages/ML libs; determinism via seeded RNG only (Date used only for version stamp / trainedAt, existing pattern); trees see only the train split (no leakage); features.ts, metrics.ts, intelligence.jsx, overview route, reminder-engine, study files, Dashboard untouched; no test files.
- Verification: `bun run lint` → 0 errors / 0 warnings; tsc filtered to changed files → only the pre-existing `y.reduce` quirk inside the untouched LR trainer (same class of pre-existing error as metrics.ts:112, which was not touched). curl POST /api/ml/train ×2 (success, 4-entry candidates array, evidence-based reason incl. small-dataset note); GET /api/ml/status → artifacts for all four types with exactly ONE isActive; GET /api/intelligence/overview → 200 with active model info; determinism PASS (identical rocAuc/brier per candidate across runs, only version stamps differ); reminder-engine tick 200 (persistPrediction path intact); throwaway script (deleted after run) confirmed the DB-stored RF/GB artifacts JSON round-trip, predict, and global-importance explain() shape.

Stage Summary:
- Four-candidate comparison live on the real stack (68 resolved events, 28 positives, temporal 47/21):
  | candidate | version | ROC-AUC | PR-AUC | Brier | logLoss | accuracy |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | baseline | baseline-v1 | 0.1184 | 0.8329 | 0.1503 | 0.5360 | 0.9048 |
  | logistic-regression | lr-v20260927070303 | 0.7368 | 0.9740 | 0.8361 | 3.6364 | 0.0952 |
  | gradient-boosting | gb-v20260927070303 | 0.4079 | 0.9041 | 0.5510 | 1.4035 | 0.0952 |
  | random-forest | rf-v20260927070303 | 0.5658 | 0.9347 | 0.2413 | 0.6752 | 0.6667 |
- PROMOTED: logistic-regression (lr-v20260927070303) — the only evidence-based winner: temporal-test ROC-AUC 0.7368 ≥ baseline 0.1184 (all three trained models actually beat-or-tied the baseline on ROC-AUC this run; LR won on ROC-AUC despite its poor Brier 0.8361, which is reported honestly — the temporal test split is heavily positive-skewed 19/21, so calibration metrics are bad for every rank-forward model). RF (Brier 0.2413, the best-calibrated trained model) and GB (ROC-AUC 0.4079) were registered as versioned inactive artifacts — evidence-based promotion kept them out, no tuning to force a winner.
- Determinism verified: two consecutive train runs produced byte-identical metrics per candidate (rocAuc/brier/prAuc identical; only version timestamps differ).
- Model versioning verified: status route lists artifacts for all four modelTypes, exactly one isActive; stale active rows across all four types deactivated on each run; run-2 correctly superseded run-1's LR version.

Difference smart − control with Newcombe method-10 95% CI; estimable:false + explicit reason naming the empty arm when either has 0 resolved high-risk doses. integrity.controlExtraSent computed FROM THE LEDGER (not trusted from the engine) with fixed note. Bilingual §18 disclaimers (behavioural demo data, not clinical evidence). modelAvailability per arm. Response wrapped in withMetrics like the intelligence routes.

Task ID: 2-a

Task: Conversation 2.0 DB-backed threads + AI response cache (v5 item 35) + daily request budget (v5 item 34) for the agent backend.

Work Log:
- Read the worklog tail (master-audit-2026-09-29 + later sessions) and audited the live code: gateway.ts pipeline, the 4 self-contained agent modules (each with a module-level `Map<userId, ConversationSummaryMemory>` — the P0 defect: keyed only by userId → cross-conversation contamination + lost on restart — plus legacy `db.conversation.create` request-log writes), providers.ts chain/breaker, tools.ts tool-call recording, http.ts error contract, the sibling route house style, and the new Prisma Conversation 2.0 model (already pushed + client regenerated by the main agent).
- NEW src/lib/server/conversations.ts — the Conversation 2.0 service: getOrCreateConversation (ownership on the WHERE clause: `id+userId+active`; foreign/archived ids silently ignored → new thread; threads may span agents — per-message `agent` records who answered), loadHistory (last 16 turns, oldest→newest), appendExchange (timestamps, title=first 60 chars of first user message, messageCount tracks the stored array, lazy LLM-free summary folding: >60 stored messages → oldest half joined into `summary` (400-char carry + 1200-char note), hard cap 200 stored messages), getSummaryForPrompt, listConversations (≤50, updatedAt desc), getConversation, archiveConversation (soft active:false); content sanitized to 8000 chars/message; agent validated against the 4 keys.
- REFACTORED all 4 agent modules (medical/medicine/personal-health/emergency-model.ts): REMOVED the memory Map, ConversationSummaryMemory/LangChain imports, the pastData db.conversation queries and ALL legacy db.conversation.create logging (AiLog in the gateway is the audit; Conversation 2.0 is the thread). Each module KEEPS its verbatim specialized prompt + Arabic instruction + its OWN provider chain via `runProviderChain(<medical|medicine|personal|emergency>, messages, {temperature:0, model?})` (per-agent separation preserved, vendor SDK coupling removed); per-agent model env overrides (MEDICAL_CHAT_MODEL etc.) preserved through ProviderCallOptions.model when explicitly set (Groq-scoped as in the original). New signature `{userId, message, language, history, summary}` + legacy aliases (`input`, `user`, `localuser`) so the vision pipeline and direct routes keep working unchanged; return body adds `text`, `latencyMs`, `fallbackUsed` alongside `output/provider/model`. personal-health keeps its own medication grounding (read-only, userId-scoped system note — original "Memory Handling" directive).
- GATEWAY (src/lib/server/ai/gateway.ts): runGateway gains `conversationId?`; after routing (incl. the emergency path) it get-or-creates the thread, injects history+summary into the specialist call, and appends the exchange post-answer with meta {agent, language, provider, modelVersion, promptVersion}; writeAiLog now returns the row id. Response body ADDITIVE fields: `conversationId`, `logId`, `cached`, `historyUsed` (0 on cache hits). Threading + append are non-fatal (DB failure never blocks an answer); degraded "service busy" outputs never pollute threads. Fixed a latent P1-40 hole in buildContext (onTimeRate null → "N/A" instead of 0%) and re-exported `type AgentKey` for the direct routes (pre-existing TS2459). wrapDirectAgentCall rebuilt its params explicitly (a client can no longer inject `message`/`history`/`summary` through the direct routes).
- AI RESPONSE CACHE (v5 item 35): globalThis-anchored LRU (cap 200, TTL 24h), key sha256(agent|language|normalizedMessage) with normalization = lowercase+trim+collapse whitespace+strip punctuation (Unicode-aware, works for Arabic). Eligible ONLY for agent ∈ {medical, medicine} ∧ intent ∈ {MEDICAL_KNOWLEDGE, MEDICATION} ∧ not emergency ∧ ZERO personal-data tool calls (ANY recorded tool — getMedications/checkInteractions/getDoseHistory/getAdherenceSummary/getHealthProfile/getStoredRiskExplanation — disqualifies: "static medical knowledge → cacheable; personal health → no shared cache" is cited in the code). Hits skip the provider call, cost no budget, still write an AiLog row (provider "cache") and still append to the conversation; misses store the FINAL post-verification output. Never cached: emergency, personal-health, vision, report, any personal-data answer.
- DAILY REQUEST BUDGET (v5 item 34): per (userId, agent) UTC-date-keyed counters, globalThis-anchored; caps env AI_DAILY_AGENT_REQUESTS (default 40) and AI_GLOBAL_AGENT_DAILY (default 5000). EMERGENCY is EXEMPT from the per-user cap on purpose (refusing to triage a possible emergency because the user chatted too much is a safety failure, not cost control — documented in-code); the global cap still protects the shared quota. Exceeded → HTTP 429 `{success:false, error:{code:"AI_BUDGET_EXCEEDED", message, limit:{perUserPerAgent, globalPerAgent, resetsAtUtcMidnight:true}}, routedTo, requestId}` matching the apiError contract shape. Budget is consumed ONLY on actual provider attempts; cache hits are free. Exported checkAiBudget/consumeAiBudget for testability.
- ROUTES: POST /api/agents accepts optional `conversationId` (string ≤64) and passes it through; NEW /api/agents/conversations (GET ?agent= list / GET ?id= detail / DELETE ?id= soft-archive) in the sibling house style (json + requireSelf + withMetrics; 400 invalid agent/params, 404 not-owned, 401 unauthenticated). - API CONTRACT for the frontend agent (Task 3-a):
  * POST /api/agents — request `{ input: string (required, ≤2000), language: "ar"|"en", preferredAgent?: "medical"|"medicine"|"personal-health"|"emergency", conversationId?: string (≤64) }` (auth: Bearer). Response 200 keeps EVERY pre-existing field (success, output, routedTo, handoffFrom, intent, severity, confidence, warnings, needs_handoff, handoff_agent, provider, model, fallbackUsed, toolCalls, requestId, latencyMs, actions, uncertainty, needsProfessionalReview, verification{result,warnings}, agentVersion, policyVersion, modelVersion, router{...}) and ADDS: `conversationId: string|null` (thread the exchange was appended to — send it back on the next message to continue the thread), `logId: string|null` (AiLog audit row), `cached: boolean` (true when served from the cache; provider is then "cache"), `historyUsed: number` (turns injected; 0 on cache hits). NEW 429 body: `{ success:false, error:{ code:"AI_BUDGET_EXCEEDED", message, limit:{ perUserPerAgent, globalPerAgent, resetsAtUtcMidnight:true } }, routedTo, requestId }`.
  * GET /api/agents/conversations?agent=medical|medicine|personal-health|emergency → 200 `{ success:true, conversations:[{ id, agent, title, messageCount, language, updatedAt, createdAt }] }` (≤50, most recent first; 400 on any other agent value).
  * GET /api/agents/conversations?id=<conversationId> → 200 `{ success:true, conversation:{ id, agent, title, language, messageCount, summary, messages:[{ role:"user"|"assistant", content, at:ISO-string, agent?:string }], createdAt, updatedAt } }`; 404 `{ success:false, message:"Conversation not found" }` when foreign/archived/unknown.
  * DELETE /api/agents/conversations?id=<conversationId> → 200 `{ success:true }` (soft archive) | 404 as above. All three require the Bearer token (401 otherwise); no params → 400.
  * Behaviour notes: history window = last 16 turns + rolling summary (threads >60 messages fold automatically); messages capped at 8000 chars; cache TTL 24h/LRU 200 (only static medical/medication knowledge with zero personal-data tools); budget defaults 40/user/agent/day + 5000 global/agent/day (env AI_DAILY_AGENT_REQUESTS / AI_GLOBAL_AGENT_DAILY; emergency exempt from the per-user cap); threads survive restarts (DB) but the CACHE and BUDGET counters are in-memory (reset on server restart — deliberate, documented).

Note: the direct specialist path never reads or writes the cache (no cacheGet/cacheSet in wrapDirectAgentCall).
- MR-16.5: ✅ + G-01 CONFIRMED ⚠️ — checkAiBudget (gateway.ts:627-639): per-(userId,agent) cap AI_DAILY_AGENT_REQUESTS (default 40) + global per-agent cap AI_GLOBAL_AGENT_DAILY (default 5000); emergency per-user EXEMPT (perUserCap=0 :629) but the global cap still applies; UTC-midnight reset via day key (:608-615, toISOString().slice(0,10)); pure check (no consume). consumeAiBudget (:643-648) runs only immediately before the provider attempt (:962) and never on cache hits (the whole budget block :931-962 sits in the non-cache else branch); exceeded → 429 AI_BUDGET_EXCEEDED with perUserPerAgent/globalPerAgent/resetsAtUtcMidnight details (:944-960). UNIT TEST: 32/32 PASS (per-user block at 2/2, per-agent counter independence, emergency per-user exemption, global-cap block at 3/3). G-01 ANSWER: YES — the direct specialist routes bypass the budget. Grep across src/: checkAiBudget/consumeAiBudget appear ONLY in gateway.ts (definitions :627/:643; sole call sites :934/:962, both inside runGateway); wrapDirectAgentCall (:1171-1332) contains NEITHER → POST /api/agents/medical|medicine|personal-health|emergency (each route file delegates to wrapDirectAgentCall: medical:19, medicine:19, personal-health:22, emergency:19) bypass BOTH the per-user and global caps; the direct emergency route also skips the global cap (the gateway path does enforce it for emergency); the VISION route does NOT budget either (zero budget references in vision/route.ts — matches MR-9's finding). Aggravating: direct calls also bypass the response cache → always a full provider call with zero budget accounting, and no chat endpoint has a per-user rate limit (only feedback does) — the daily budget is the ONLY cost guard, and it is absent on the direct + vision paths. Mitigations that DO apply on direct routes: requireSelf, safetyCheck emergency override, buildContext, verifyOutput, AiLog audit, 2000-char message cap.
- Gaps found: (1) G-01 CONFIRMED (defect, cost/abuse surface): direct specialist routes (medical/medicine/personal-health/emergency via wrapDirectAgentCall) AND the vision route bypass checkAiBudget — gateway.ts:934 is the only budget gate in the codebase. Central-fix suggestion: call checkAiBudget/consumeAiBudget inside wrapDirectAgentCall and the vision route with the same 429 AI_BUDGET_EXCEEDED contract. (2) AiFeedback has no DB unique on (userId,aiLogId) — upsert is application-level (feedback/route.ts:123-135); a concurrent double-submit could double-insert (low probability behind the 30/10min limit). (3) Feedback update keeps the first submission's agent/conversationId (route.ts:126-129) — later values silently ignored. (4) Budget + cache stores are in-memory globalThis — counters reset on restart and are per-instance in multi-instance deploys (documented design; single-instance sandbox unaffected). (5) Informational: getOrCreateConversation isolates on (userId+conversationId+active) without agent — deliberate (span-agent threads) and safe, but differs from the plan's "userId+agent+conversationId" phrasing; (userId,agent) scoping is provided by listConversations. (6) Informational: the shared 200-entry LRU has no per-user quota — one user could churn cache entries (self-evicting, low impact).
